# Benchmarks - NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti + NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti on Intel(R) Core(TM) i9-10900K CPU @ 3.70GHz

803 run(s), 2026-09-03 to 2026-10-05. loken builds measured: 0.1.0-dev, 0.2.0-dev. A row's `Date` and `Run` name the run behind it. 30 replayable record(s) of this hardware are in the store and not tabled. 631 archived run(s) (superseded builds or another protocol) are held and not tabled.

## Hardware

| | |
|---|---|
| GPU 0 | NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti - 16 GB, 300 W |
| GPU 1 | NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti - 16 GB, 180 W |
| CPU | Intel(R) Core(TM) i9-10900K CPU @ 3.70GHz - 20 threads |
| RAM | 62 GB |
| Backend | unstated |
| Driver | 610.43.02 |
| Kernel | 6.12.90+deb13.1-amd64 |

Runs taken before the runner recorded its machine carry the hardware an operator attested for them; their driver and kernel are unknown.

## Results

Method and column definitions: [BENCHMARKS.md](../BENCHMARKS.md). Each rate reads `cold / warm`: the first request of a cell, then the median of the later ones ([why](../BENCHMARKS.md#cold-and-warm)).
